FILE .github/workflows/ci.yml
name: CI



on:

  workflow_dispatch:

  push:

    branches: [main]

  pull_request:

    branches: [main]



permissions:

  contents: read



jobs:

  test:

    name: Python ${{ matrix.python }}${{ matrix.experimental && ' (experimental)' || '' }}

    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    continue-on-error: ${{ matrix.experimental }}

    strategy:

      fail-fast: false

      matrix:

        include:

          - python: "3.13"

            experimental: false

          - python: "3.14"

            experimental: true

    steps:

      - uses: actions/checkout@v7

      - uses: astral-sh/setup-uv@v7

        with:

          version: "0.12.1"

          enable-cache: true

      - name: Install locked environment

        run: uv sync --frozen --python ${{ matrix.python }} --group dev --group architecture --extra http --extra scraping

      - name: Scientific architecture boundary

        run: uv run --no-sync lint-imports

      - name: Test

        run: |

          uv run --no-sync coverage erase

          uv run --no-sync coverage run --source=src/predictor_core -m pytest -q

          uv run --no-sync coverage report --fail-under=80

      - name: Lint and format

        run: uv run --no-sync ruff check . && uv run --no-sync ruff format --check .

      - name: Type check

        run: uv run --no-sync pyright



  wheel:

    name: Build and test wheel outside checkout

    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    steps:

      - uses: actions/checkout@v7

      - uses: astral-sh/setup-uv@v7

        with:

          version: "0.12.1"

      - run: uv build --wheel

      - name: Install wheel in external directory and process

        shell: bash

        run: |

          temp_dir="$(mktemp -d)"

          uv venv --python 3.13 "$temp_dir/.venv"

          uv pip install --python "$temp_dir/.venv/bin/python" dist/*.whl

          wheel="$(realpath dist/*.whl)"

          cp tools/check_installed_wheel.py "$temp_dir/check_installed_wheel.py"

          cd "$temp_dir"

          "$temp_dir/.venv/bin/python" -I check_installed_wheel.py --wheel "$wheel"



  audit:

    name: Security and distribution audit

    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    steps:

      - uses: actions/checkout@v7

      - name: Reject cross-repository push automation

        shell: bash

        run: |

          token_pattern="SYNC_"'PAT_TOKEN'

          push_pattern="git "'push'

          if grep -R -E "$token_pattern|$push_pattern" .github/workflows; then

            echo 'Cross-repository push automation is forbidden.' >&2

            exit 1

          fi


FILE .github/workflows/consumer-compatibility.yml
name: Pinned consumer compatibility



on:

  workflow_dispatch:

  push:

    branches: [main]

  pull_request:

    branches: [main]

    paths:

      - '.github/workflows/consumer-compatibility.yml'

      - 'tools/compatibility_candidate.json'



permissions:

  contents: read



jobs:

  plugins:

    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    timeout-minutes: 20

    steps:

      - uses: actions/checkout@v7

      - uses: actions/checkout@v7

        with:

          repository: leonardosovienski/ecosystem-predictor

          ref: ae74476d66b7a1e43c635b95429eec1cb4460d17

          path: integration-ecosystem

      - uses: actions/setup-python@v5

        with:

          python-version: '3.13'

      - name: Install exact consumer sources and distributed shared wheels

        working-directory: integration-ecosystem

        run: |

          python -m venv "$RUNNER_TEMP/compatibility"

          python scripts/install_compatibility_candidate.py --python "$RUNNER_TEMP/compatibility/bin/python" --manifest ../tools/compatibility_candidate.json

      - name: Verify real plugin behavior, hashes and isolation

        working-directory: integration-ecosystem

        env:

          CRIPTO_ROOT: ${{ runner.temp }}/crypto-integration

          COMPATIBILITY_RECEIPT: ${{ runner.temp }}/compatibility-receipt.json

        run: |

          "$RUNNER_TEMP/compatibility/bin/python" scripts/check_real_plugin_integration.py --manifest ../tools/compatibility_candidate.json

          "$RUNNER_TEMP/compatibility/bin/python" -m pip check

          "$RUNNER_TEMP/compatibility/bin/python" -m pip freeze > "$RUNNER_TEMP/compatibility-dependencies.txt"

      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4

        if: always()

        with:

          name: pinned-consumer-compatibility

          path: |

            ${{ runner.temp }}/compatibility-receipt.json

            ${{ runner.temp }}/compatibility-dependencies.txt

          if-no-files-found: warn


FILE .github/workflows/dependency-review.yml
name: Dependency Review



on:

  pull_request:

    branches: [main]



permissions:

  contents: read



concurrency:

  group: dependency-review-${{ github.event.pull_request.number }}

  cancel-in-progress: true



jobs:

  dependency-review:

    name: Dependency Review

    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    timeout-minutes: 10



    steps:

      - name: Checkout repository

        uses: actions/checkout@v7



      - name: Review dependency changes

        uses: actions/dependency-review-action@v5

        with:

          fail-on-severity: high

          fail-on-scopes: runtime, development, unknown

          vulnerability-check: true

          license-check: true

          show-patched-versions: true


FILE .github/workflows/release.yml
name: Release



on:

  workflow_dispatch:

  push:

    tags:

      - "v*.*.*"



permissions:

  contents: read



concurrency:

  group: release-${{ github.ref }}

  cancel-in-progress: false



jobs:

  build:

    name: Build and validate package

    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    timeout-minutes: 20



    permissions:

      contents: read

      id-token: write

      attestations: write



    steps:

      - name: Checkout repository

        uses: actions/checkout@v7



      - name: Set up uv

        uses: astral-sh/setup-uv@v7

        with:

          version: "0.12.1"

          enable-cache: true



      - name: Install Python

        run: uv python install 3.13



      - name: Install locked environment

        run: uv sync --frozen --python 3.13 --group dev --extra http --extra scraping



      - name: Validate tag against project version

        if: startsWith(github.ref, 'refs/tags/')

        shell: python

        run: |

          import os

          import pathlib

          import tomllib



          tag = os.environ["GITHUB_REF_NAME"]

          expected_version = tag.removeprefix("v")



          with pathlib.Path("pyproject.toml").open("rb") as file:

              project = tomllib.load(file)



          actual_version = project["project"]["version"]



          if actual_version != expected_version:

              raise SystemExit(

                  f"Tag {tag!r} does not match pyproject.toml "

                  f"version {actual_version!r}"

              )



          print(f"Release version validated: {actual_version}")



      - name: Run tests with coverage

        run: |

          uv run --no-sync coverage erase

          uv run --no-sync coverage run --source=src/predictor_core -m pytest -q

          uv run --no-sync coverage report --fail-under=80



      - name: Run lint and format checks

        run: |

          uv run --no-sync ruff check .

          uv run --no-sync ruff format --check .



      - name: Run type check

        run: uv run --no-sync pyright



      - name: Audit dependencies

        run: uvx pip-audit



      - name: Build wheel and source distribution

        run: uv build



      - name: Test wheel in isolated environment

        shell: bash

        run: |

          isolated="$(mktemp -d)"

          uv venv --python 3.13 "$isolated/.venv"

          uv pip install --python "$isolated/.venv/bin/python" dist/*.whl

          wheel="$(realpath dist/*.whl)"

          cp tools/check_installed_wheel.py "$isolated/check_installed_wheel.py"

          cd "$isolated"

          "$isolated/.venv/bin/python" -I check_installed_wheel.py --wheel "$wheel"



      - name: Generate artifact attestations

        if: startsWith(github.ref, 'refs/tags/')

        uses: actions/attest@v4

        with:

          subject-path: |

            dist/*.whl

            dist/*.tar.gz



      - name: Upload package artifact

        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v7

        with:

          name: predictor-core-${{ github.run_id }}

          path: dist/*

          if-no-files-found: error

          retention-days: 14



  release:

    name: Publish GitHub Release

    if: startsWith(github.ref, 'refs/tags/')

    needs: build

    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    timeout-minutes: 10



    permissions:

      contents: write



    steps:

      - name: Download package artifact

        uses: actions/download-artifact@v8

        with:

          name: predictor-core-${{ github.run_id }}

          path: dist



      - name: Create or update GitHub Release

        env:

          GH_TOKEN: ${{ github.token }}

        shell: bash

        run: |

          if gh release view "${GITHUB_REF_NAME}" \

            --repo "${GITHUB_REPOSITORY}" >/dev/null 2>&1; then



            echo "Release ${GITHUB_REF_NAME} already exists."

            echo "Uploading or replacing package assets."



            gh release upload "${GITHUB_REF_NAME}" \

              dist/* \

              --repo "${GITHUB_REPOSITORY}" \

              --clobber

          else

            echo "Creating release ${GITHUB_REF_NAME}."



            gh release create "${GITHUB_REF_NAME}" \

              dist/* \

              --repo "${GITHUB_REPOSITORY}" \

              --title "${GITHUB_REF_NAME}" \

              --generate-notes \

              --verify-tag

          fi

def __getattr__(name: str):
    if name not in _EXPORTS:
        raise AttributeError(f"module {__name__!r} has no attribute {name!r}")
    module, attribute = _EXPORTS[name]
    value = getattr(import_module(module), attribute)
    globals()[name] = value
    return value


def __dir__():
    return sorted(set(globals()) | set(__all__))

FILE src/predictor_core/contracts/__init__.py
"""Compatibility facade; new code should import explicit submodule APIs.

Importing the package does not load networking, persistence or measurement.
Legacy public names remain available through lazy attribute resolution.
"""

from importlib import import_module
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from predictor_core.contracts.collection import (  # noqa: F401
        COLLECTION_SCHEMA_VERSION,
        CollectionArchive,

def __getattr__(name: str):
    if name not in _EXPORTS:
        raise AttributeError(f"module {__name__!r} has no attribute {name!r}")
    module, attribute = _EXPORTS[name]
    value = getattr(import_module(module), attribute)
    globals()[name] = value
    return value


def __dir__():
    return sorted(set(globals()) | set(__all__))
